| PO Naming Convention  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Any load from AECN will have the following PO convention  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| 001T1R0 | \- (001) | This refers to the total number of POs, and will increase with each new PO generated  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| truck to rail  | \- T1 | This refers to the truck that is being loaded at AECN, and for long haul truck loads, this will always be T1, for loads going rail, this will increase with the number of trucks it takes to fill a railcar  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | \- R1 | This refers to the number railcar that we are filling with trucks, and will increase with each railload starting at 1  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Any load from Maize, KS to any site will have the following PO convention  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| 001R1T1 | \- (001) | This refers to the total number of POs, and will increase with each new PO generated  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| rail to truck  | \- R1 | This refers to the number railcar that we are unloading with trucks, and will increase with each railload starting at 1  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | \- T1 | This refers to the truck that is being loaded at Maize, KS location, and for loads coming from rail, this will increase with the number of trucks it takes to empty a railcar  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| Any load from any location directly to Vaulted will have the following PO convention  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
| 001V  | \- (001) | This refers to the total number of POs, and will increase with each new PO generated  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  | \- V | This refers to the load going to Vauled  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
|  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |  |
